# Embedded original code

Generated by `re/g_embed.py` from `libcns_dump_64.dll`. Every block below is domain code this work read but has
**not** reimplemented in C++; its original bytes now live in the project, and `re/check_embeddings.py` re-reads
the DLL and fails if any byte or hash drifts.

A block is **callable** when it has no RIP-relative memory operand, no call or jump leaving it and no absolute
image address: then it is position-independent and the test can call it, which is what makes a differential test
against a C++ reimplementation possible. Otherwise it is **comment_only**, with the reason recorded.
